// [FILE_START]: HTNOperators.cs
// PATH: AI_Core\HTNOperators.cs
// ------------------------------------------

﻿using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.AI;
using FluidHTN;
using FluidHTN.Operators;
using System;

// ==========================================================
// 1. CHỌN ĐIỂM TUẦN TRA 
// ==========================================================
public class GetNextWaypointOperator : IOperator
{
    public TaskStatus Start(IContext ctx)
    {
        AiContext context = (AiContext)ctx;
        if (context.PatrolPath == null || context.PatrolPath.WaypointCount == 0) return TaskStatus.Failure;

        context.CurrentMoveDestination = context.PatrolPath.GetWaypointPosition(context.CurrentWaypointIndex);
        context.CurrentWaypointIndex = (context.CurrentWaypointIndex + 1) % context.PatrolPath.WaypointCount;

        return TaskStatus.Success;
    }

    public TaskStatus Update(IContext ctx) => TaskStatus.Success;
    public void Stop(IContext ctx) { }
    public void Abort(IContext ctx) { }
}

// ==========================================================
// 2. XOAY THÂN NGƯỜI (CÓ CẦU CHÌ BẢO VỆ CỔ)
// ==========================================================
public class TurnToFaceOperator : IOperator
{
    private bool _isInvestigateMode;
    private Func<AiContext, float> _getTurnSpeed;

    public TurnToFaceOperator(bool isInvestigateMode, Func<AiContext, float> getTurnSpeed)
    {
        _isInvestigateMode = isInvestigateMode;
        _getTurnSpeed = getTurnSpeed;
    }

    public TaskStatus Start(IContext ctx)
    {
        AiContext context = (AiContext)ctx;
        if (context.Agent?.NavAgent != null && context.Agent.NavAgent.isActiveAndEnabled && context.Agent.NavAgent.isOnNavMesh)
        {
            context.Agent.NavAgent.ResetPath();
            context.Agent.NavAgent.updateRotation = false;
        }
        return TaskStatus.Continue;
    }

    public TaskStatus Update(IContext ctx)
    {
        AiContext context = (AiContext)ctx;
        Vector3 targetPos = context.CurrentMoveDestination;

        if (_isInvestigateMode)
        {
            targetPos = (context.HasState(AIWorldState.HasTarget) && context.Target != null)
                ? context.Target.position : context.SuspectedLocation;
        }

        if (Vector3.Distance(context.Agent.transform.position, targetPos) > 1.5f)
            context.LookFocusOverride = targetPos + Vector3.up * 1.2f;
        else
            context.LookFocusOverride = null;

        Vector3 dir = (targetPos - context.Agent.transform.position).normalized;
        dir.y = 0;
        if (dir == Vector3.zero)
        {
            context.LookFocusOverride = null;
            return TaskStatus.Success;
        }

        Quaternion lookRot = Quaternion.LookRotation(dir);

        // VI: Ép tốc độ xoay (nhân hệ số 20 để chuyển đổi logic Speed cũ sang Độ/Giây)
        // EN: Force rotation speed (multiply by 20 to convert old Speed logic to Degrees/Second)
        float turnSpeedInDegrees = _getTurnSpeed(context) * 20f;
        context.Agent.transform.rotation = Quaternion.RotateTowards(context.Agent.transform.rotation, lookRot, turnSpeedInDegrees * Time.deltaTime);

        if (Quaternion.Angle(context.Agent.transform.rotation, lookRot) <= 5f)
        {
            context.LookFocusOverride = null;
            return TaskStatus.Success;
        }
        return TaskStatus.Continue;
    }

    public void Stop(IContext ctx)
    {
        AiContext context = (AiContext)ctx;
        context.LookFocusOverride = null;
        if (context.Agent?.NavAgent != null) context.Agent.NavAgent.updateRotation = true;
    }
    public void Abort(IContext ctx) => Stop(ctx);
}

// ==========================================================
// 3. DI CHUYỂN CÓ MỤC ĐÍCH
// ==========================================================
public class MoveToVectorDestinationOperator : IOperator
{
    private bool _isInvestigateMode;
    private Func<AiContext, float> _getStoppingDistance;
    private bool _isDestinationSet = false;

    public MoveToVectorDestinationOperator(bool isInvestigateMode, Func<AiContext, float> getStoppingDistance)
    {
        _isInvestigateMode = isInvestigateMode;
        _getStoppingDistance = getStoppingDistance;
    }

    public TaskStatus Start(IContext ctx)
    {
        _isDestinationSet = false;
        AiContext context = (AiContext)ctx;
        if (context.Agent?.NavAgent != null) context.Agent.NavAgent.updateRotation = true;
        return TaskStatus.Continue;
    }

    public TaskStatus Update(IContext ctx)
    {
        AiContext context = (AiContext)ctx;
        if (context.Agent == null || context.Agent.NavAgent == null) return TaskStatus.Failure;

        if (!_isDestinationSet)
        {
            Vector3 targetTarget = _isInvestigateMode ?
                ((context.HasState(AIWorldState.HasTarget) && context.Target != null) ? context.Target.position : context.SuspectedLocation)
                : context.CurrentMoveDestination;
            context.Agent.NavAgent.stoppingDistance = _getStoppingDistance(context);
            context.Agent.NavAgent.SetDestination(targetTarget);
            _isDestinationSet = true;
        }

        if (!context.Agent.NavAgent.pathPending && context.Agent.NavAgent.remainingDistance <= context.Agent.NavAgent.stoppingDistance)
        {
            _isDestinationSet = false;
            return TaskStatus.Success;
        }
        return TaskStatus.Continue;
    }

    public void Stop(IContext ctx)
    {
        AiContext context = (AiContext)ctx;
        if (context.Agent?.NavAgent != null && context.Agent.NavAgent.isActiveAndEnabled && context.Agent.NavAgent.isOnNavMesh)
        {
            context.Agent.NavAgent.ResetPath();
        }
        _isDestinationSet = false;
    }
    public void Abort(IContext ctx) => Stop(ctx);
}

// ==========================================================
// 4. CHỜ ĐỢI
// ==========================================================
public class WaitOperator : IOperator
{
    private Func<AiContext, float> _getWaitTime;
    private float _timer;
    public WaitOperator(Func<AiContext, float> getWaitTime) { _getWaitTime = getWaitTime; }
    public TaskStatus Start(IContext ctx) { _timer = 0f; return TaskStatus.Continue; }
    public TaskStatus Update(IContext ctx)
    {
        _timer += Time.deltaTime;
        return _timer >= _getWaitTime((AiContext)ctx) ? TaskStatus.Success : TaskStatus.Continue;
    }
    public void Stop(IContext ctx) { }
    public void Abort(IContext ctx) => Stop(ctx);
}

// ==========================================================
// 5. TELEGRAPH (ĐỨNG NHÌN)
// ==========================================================
public class TelegraphOperator : IOperator
{
    private bool _isInvestigateMode;
    private Func<AiContext, float> _getTelegraphTime;
    private float _timer;

    public TelegraphOperator(bool isInvestigateMode, Func<AiContext, float> getTelegraphTime)
    {
        _isInvestigateMode = isInvestigateMode;
        _getTelegraphTime = getTelegraphTime;
    }

    public TaskStatus Start(IContext ctx)
    {
        AiContext context = (AiContext)ctx;
        _timer = 0f;

        if (context.Agent?.NavAgent != null && context.Agent.NavAgent.isActiveAndEnabled && context.Agent.NavAgent.isOnNavMesh)
        {
            context.Agent.NavAgent.ResetPath();
        }

        Vector3 targetPos = _isInvestigateMode ?
            ((context.HasState(AIWorldState.HasTarget) && context.Target != null) ? context.Target.position : context.SuspectedLocation)
            : context.CurrentMoveDestination;

        // Cầu chì an toàn
        if (Vector3.Distance(context.Agent.transform.position, targetPos) > 1.5f)
            context.LookFocusOverride = targetPos + Vector3.up * 1.2f;
        else
            context.LookFocusOverride = null;

        return TaskStatus.Continue;
    }

    public TaskStatus Update(IContext ctx)
    {
        _timer += Time.deltaTime;
        if (_timer >= _getTelegraphTime((AiContext)ctx))
        {
            ((AiContext)ctx).LookFocusOverride = null;
            return TaskStatus.Success;
        }
        return TaskStatus.Continue;
    }

    public void Stop(IContext ctx) { ((AiContext)ctx).LookFocusOverride = null; }
    public void Abort(IContext ctx) => Stop(ctx);
}

// ==========================================================
// 6. RÀ QUÉT KHU VỰC (SẠCH SẼ, KHÔNG HACK TỌA ĐỘ)
// ==========================================================
public class ScanAreaOperator : IOperator
{
    private System.Func<AiContext, float> _getDuration;
    private float _timer;

    public ScanAreaOperator(System.Func<AiContext, float> getDuration)
    {
        _getDuration = getDuration;
    }

    public TaskStatus Start(IContext ctx)
    {
        var context = (AiContext)ctx;
        _timer = 0f;

        if (context.Agent.NavAgent != null && context.Agent.NavAgent.isOnNavMesh)
        {
            context.Agent.NavAgent.ResetPath();
            context.Agent.NavAgent.isStopped = true;
        }

        // CHỈ set IsScanning = true. AILookController sẽ tự động đảo mắt radar!
        context.IsScanning = true;

        return TaskStatus.Continue;
    }

    public TaskStatus Update(IContext ctx)
    {
        _timer += Time.deltaTime;

        if (_timer >= _getDuration((AiContext)ctx))
        {
            CleanUp((AiContext)ctx);
            return TaskStatus.Success;
        }
        return TaskStatus.Continue;
    }

    public void Stop(IContext ctx) => CleanUp((AiContext)ctx);
    public void Abort(IContext ctx) => CleanUp((AiContext)ctx);

    private void CleanUp(AiContext context)
    {
        context.IsScanning = false;

        if (context.Agent.NavAgent != null && context.Agent.NavAgent.isOnNavMesh)
        {
            context.Agent.NavAgent.isStopped = false;
        }
    }
}

// ==========================================================
// 7. [COMBAT] VÂY RÁP MỤC TIÊU 
// ==========================================================
public class CircleTargetOperator : IOperator
{
    private Func<AiContext, float> _getRadius;
    private bool _isMoving;
    private float _waitTimer;

    public CircleTargetOperator(Func<AiContext, float> getRadius) { _getRadius = getRadius; }

    public TaskStatus Start(IContext ctx)
    {
        AiContext context = (AiContext)ctx;
        if (context.Target == null) return TaskStatus.Failure;

        if (context.Agent?.NavAgent != null && context.Agent.NavAgent.isOnNavMesh)
        {
            context.Agent.NavAgent.isStopped = false;
            context.Agent.NavAgent.stoppingDistance = 0f;
            context.Agent.NavAgent.speed = context.Agent.TacticalSettings.prowlSpeed;
        }

        FindNewCirclePoint(context);
        return TaskStatus.Continue;
    }

    public TaskStatus Update(IContext ctx)
    {
        AiContext context = (AiContext)ctx;
        if (context.Target == null || context.Agent?.NavAgent == null) return TaskStatus.Failure;

        context.LookFocusOverride = context.Target.position + Vector3.up * 1.2f;

        if (_isMoving)
        {
            context.Agent.NavAgent.updateRotation = true;
            if (!context.Agent.NavAgent.pathPending && context.Agent.NavAgent.remainingDistance <= 1f)
            {
                _isMoving = false;
                _waitTimer = UnityEngine.Random.Range(0.2f, 0.6f);

                if (context.Agent.NavAgent.isActiveAndEnabled && context.Agent.NavAgent.isOnNavMesh)
                {
                    context.Agent.NavAgent.ResetPath();
                }
            }
        }
        // ...
        else
        {
            context.Agent.NavAgent.updateRotation = false;
            Vector3 dir = (context.Target.position - context.Agent.transform.position).normalized;
            dir.y = 0;
            if (dir != Vector3.zero)
            {
                Quaternion lookRot = Quaternion.LookRotation(dir);
                context.Agent.transform.rotation = Quaternion.Slerp(context.Agent.transform.rotation, lookRot, Time.deltaTime * context.Agent.TacticalSettings.combatTurnSpeed);
            }

            _waitTimer -= Time.deltaTime;
            if (_waitTimer <= 0f) FindNewCirclePoint(context);
        }
        return TaskStatus.Continue;
    }

    private void FindNewCirclePoint(AiContext context)
    {
        Vector3 dirFromPlayer = (context.Agent.transform.position - context.Target.position).normalized;
        float randomAngle = UnityEngine.Random.Range(45f, 90f) * (UnityEngine.Random.value > 0.5f ? 1f : -1f);
        Vector3 rotatedDir = Quaternion.Euler(0, randomAngle, 0) * dirFromPlayer;
        Vector3 targetPoint = context.Target.position + (rotatedDir * _getRadius(context));

        if (NavMesh.SamplePosition(targetPoint, out NavMeshHit hit, 5f, NavMesh.AllAreas))
        {
            context.Agent.NavAgent.isStopped = false;
            context.Agent.NavAgent.SetDestination(hit.position);
            _isMoving = true;
        }
        else
        {
            _isMoving = false;
            _waitTimer = 1f;
        }
    }

    public void Stop(IContext ctx)
    {
        AiContext context = (AiContext)ctx;
        context.LookFocusOverride = null;
        if (context.Agent?.NavAgent != null && context.Agent.NavAgent.isOnNavMesh)
        {
            context.Agent.NavAgent.ResetPath();
            context.Agent.NavAgent.updateRotation = true;
        }
    }
    public void Abort(IContext ctx) => Stop(ctx);
}

// ==========================================================
// 8. [COMBAT] KHUNG SƯỜN TUNG ĐÒN UTILITY AI (GIAO TIẾP VỚI CONTROLLER)
// ==========================================================
public class UtilityCombatOperator : IOperator
{
    private AISkillController _skillController;

    public TaskStatus Start(IContext ctx)
    {
        AiContext context = (AiContext)ctx;
        if (context.Target == null) return TaskStatus.Failure;

        _skillController = context.Agent.GetComponent<AISkillController>();
        if (_skillController == null) return TaskStatus.Failure;

        // Ra lệnh cho Controller thực thi chiêu tốt nhất
        _skillController.StartBestSkill(context.Target);

        // Đọc trạng thái (Nếu StartBestSkill trả về Failure ngay lập tức vì kẹt FOV/hết đạn)
        return _skillController.CurrentSkillStatus;
    }

    public TaskStatus Update(IContext ctx)
    {
        // HTN chỉ ngồi chơi xơi nước và lắng nghe Controller báo cáo
        return _skillController.CurrentSkillStatus;
    }

    public void Stop(IContext ctx)
    {
        // Bị ngắt ngang (Do Stagger) -> Ép Controller dọn dẹp
        if (_skillController != null) _skillController.AbortExecution(TaskStatus.Failure);
    }

    public void Abort(IContext ctx) => Stop(ctx);
}

// [FILE_END]: HTNOperators.cs
// ==========================================


// [FILE_START]: ProximitySensor.cs
// PATH: AI_Core\ProximitySensor.cs
// ------------------------------------------

﻿using UnityEngine;
using FluidHTN;

[RequireComponent(typeof(AIAgent))]
public class ProximitySensor : MonoBehaviour
{
    [Header("Proximity Settings")]
    public float meleeRadius = 2.5f;
    public LayerMask playerLayerMask;

    private AIAgent _agent;
    private Collider[] _hitColliders = new Collider[5];
    private float _scanInterval = 0.25f;
    private float _timer = 0f;

    void Awake()
    {
        _agent = GetComponent<AIAgent>();
    }

    void Update()
    {
        _timer += Time.deltaTime;
        if (_timer >= _scanInterval)
        {
            _timer = 0f;

            int hits = Physics.OverlapSphereNonAlloc(transform.position, meleeRadius, _hitColliders, playerLayerMask);
            if (hits > 0)
            {
                // SỬA LỖI BIÊN DỊCH: Truy xuất phần tử [0] của mảng thay vì gọi thẳng mảng!
                Transform player = _hitColliders[0].transform;

                _agent.Context.Target = player;
                _agent.Context.SetState(AIWorldState.HasTarget, true, EffectType.Permanent);
                _agent.Context.SetState(AIWorldState.IsAlerted, true, EffectType.Permanent);

                // Kích hoạt Hive Mind cho cả bầy đàn vây bắt
                if (_agent.Context.Group != null)
                {
                    _agent.Context.Group.BroadcastPlayerLocation(player.position);
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

// [FILE_END]: ProximitySensor.cs
// ==========================================


// [FILE_START]: VisionSensor.cs
// PATH: AI_Core\VisionSensor.cs
// ------------------------------------------

﻿using UnityEngine;
using FluidHTN;

[RequireComponent(typeof(AIAgent))]
public class VisionSensor : MonoBehaviour
{
    [Header("--- EYE SOURCE (AAA) ---")]
    public Transform eyeSource;
    [Space]

    [Header("Vision Settings")]
    public float viewRadius = 30f;
    [Range(0, 360)] public float viewAngle = 90f;
    public LayerMask playerLayerMask;
    public LayerMask obstacleLayerMask;

    [Header("--- AWARENESS METER (STEALTH) ---")]
    public float timeToDetect = 1.5f;
    public float timeToLose = 4.0f;
    private float _detectionLevel = 0f;

    private AIAgent _agent;
    private Collider[] _hitColliders = new Collider[8];
    private float _scanInterval = 0.1f; // Tăng tốc độ quét lên 10 lần/s để làm mượt thanh nhận thức
    private float _timer = 0f;

    void Awake()
    {
        _agent = GetComponent<AIAgent>();
        if (eyeSource == null) eyeSource = transform;
    }

    void Update()
    {
        _timer += Time.deltaTime;
        if (_timer >= _scanInterval)
        {
            _timer = 0f;
            ProcessVisionEscalation();
        }
    }

    private void ProcessVisionEscalation()
    {
        bool isSeeingPlayer = CheckLineOfSight(out Transform playerTarget);

        if (isSeeingPlayer)
        {
            _detectionLevel += _scanInterval / timeToDetect;
            if (_detectionLevel > 1f) _detectionLevel = 1f;

            // MỨC 1 (MẮT VÀNG): Thấy lờ mờ -> Đẩy lên Group xin vé điều tra tập trung
            if (_detectionLevel > 0f && _detectionLevel < 1f && !_agent.Context.HasState(AIWorldState.HasTarget))
            {
                if (_agent.Context.Group != null)
                {
                    _agent.Context.Group.ReportDisturbance(playerTarget.position, _agent);
                }
            }

            // MỨC 2 (MẮT ĐỎ): Xác nhận 100% -> Vào Combat -> Hét gọi bầy
            if (_detectionLevel >= 1f && !_agent.Context.HasState(AIWorldState.HasTarget))
            {
                _agent.Context.SetTarget(playerTarget);

                if (_agent.Context.Group != null)
                {
                    _agent.Context.Group.BroadcastPlayerLocation(playerTarget.position);
                }
            }
        }
        else
        {
            // MẤT DẤU (Cool-down)
            if (_detectionLevel > 0f)
            {
                _detectionLevel -= _scanInterval / timeToLose;
                if (_detectionLevel <= 0f)
                {
                    _detectionLevel = 0f;
                    // Nếu đang trong giao tranh mà thanh nhận diện về 0 -> Kích hoạt Sục sạo
                    if (_agent.Context.HasState(AIWorldState.HasTarget))
                    {
                        // [THÊM MỚI DÒNG NÀY CHÍNH XÁC Ở ĐÂY]: 
                        // Bơm vị trí cuối cùng của Player vào bộ nhớ trước khi xóa Target!
                        if (_agent.Context.Target != null)
                            _agent.Context.SuspectedLocation = _agent.Context.Target.position;

                        _agent.Context.SetTarget(null);
                        _agent.Context.SetState(AIWorldState.IsSearching, true, EffectType.Permanent);
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    private bool CheckLineOfSight(out Transform foundPlayer)
    {
        foundPlayer = null;
        int hits = Physics.OverlapSphereNonAlloc(eyeSource.position, viewRadius, _hitColliders, playerLayerMask);

        for (int i = 0; i < hits; i++)
        {
            Transform target = _hitColliders[i].transform;
            Vector3 dirToTarget = (target.position - eyeSource.position).normalized;

            if (Vector3.Dot(eyeSource.forward, dirToTarget) > Mathf.Cos(viewAngle * 0.5f * Mathf.Deg2Rad))
            {
                Vector3 eyePos = eyeSource.position;
                Vector3 targetPos = target.position + Vector3.up * 1.0f;

                if (!Physics.Linecast(eyePos, targetPos, obstacleLayerMask))
                {
                    PlayerStealth stealth = target.GetComponent<PlayerStealth>();
                    bool isPlayerInGrass = stealth != null && stealth.IsHidingInGrass;

                    if (isPlayerInGrass && _detectionLevel < 1f)
                    {
                        continue;
                    }

                    foundPlayer = target;
                    return true;
                }
            }
        }
        return false;
    }
}

// [FILE_END]: VisionSensor.cs
// ==========================================


// [FILE_START]: BossBodyPart.cs
// PATH: AI_Core\Boss\BossBodyPart.cs
// ------------------------------------------

﻿using UnityEngine;

// VI: Vẫn dùng IDamageable để đạn của Player hay mảnh vỡ nhận diện được
// EN: Still uses IDamageable so Player bullets or debris can recognize it
public class BossBodyPart : MonoBehaviour, IDamageable
{
    [Tooltip("VI: Kéo script BossHealth ở object cha (Root) vào đây.\nEN: Drag the BossHealth script from the parent (Root) object here.")]
    public BossHealth mainBossHealth;

    [Tooltip("VI: Hệ số sát thương (Giáp thân = 0.2, Đuôi/Mắt = 1.5).\nEN: Damage multiplier (Body armor = 0.2, Tail/Eyes = 1.5).")]
    public float damageMultiplier = 1.0f;

    public void TakeDamage(float damage, Vector3 hitPoint, Vector3 hitNormal = default)
    {
        if (mainBossHealth == null) return;

        // VI: Nhân sát thương và truyền về cục gốc
        // EN: Multiply damage and pass it to the root
        float finalDamage = damage * damageMultiplier;
        mainBossHealth.TakeDamage(finalDamage, hitPoint, hitNormal);
    }
}

// [FILE_START]: BossHealth.cs
// PATH: AI_Core\Boss\BossHealth.cs
// ------------------------------------------

﻿using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.UI;

public class BossHealth : MonoBehaviour, IDamageable
{
    [Header("--- BOSS STATS ---")]
    public float maxHealth = 3000f;
    public float currentHealth;
    private bool _isDead = false;

    [Header("--- UI HEALTH BAR ---")]
    public Slider frontHealthBar;
    public Slider backHealthBar;

    [Header("--- GHOST BAR SETTINGS ---")]
    public float ghostDelay = 0.5f;
    public float ghostLerpSpeed = 5f;
    private float _ghostTimer = 0f;

    void Awake()
    {
        currentHealth = maxHealth;
        if (frontHealthBar != null) { frontHealthBar.maxValue = maxHealth; frontHealthBar.value = currentHealth; }
        if (backHealthBar != null) { backHealthBar.maxValue = maxHealth; backHealthBar.value = currentHealth; }
    }

    void Update()
    {
        if (_isDead || frontHealthBar == null || backHealthBar == null) return;
        frontHealthBar.value = currentHealth;

        if (backHealthBar.value > frontHealthBar.value)
        {
            _ghostTimer += Time.deltaTime;
            if (_ghostTimer > ghostDelay)
            {
                backHealthBar.value = Mathf.Lerp(backHealthBar.value, frontHealthBar.value, ghostLerpSpeed * Time.deltaTime);
            }
        }
    }

    public void TakeDamage(float damage, Vector3 hitPoint, Vector3 hitNormal = default)
    {
        if (_isDead) return;
        currentHealth -= damage;
        _ghostTimer = 0f;

        if (currentHealth <= 0)
        {
            currentHealth = 0;
            if (frontHealthBar != null) frontHealthBar.value = 0;
            Die();
        }
    }

    private void Die()
    {
        if (_isDead) return;
        _isDead = true;

        Debug.Log("<color=yellow>[BOSS] ĐÃ BỊ TIÊU DIỆT!</color>");

        BossSplineAttacker splineMovement = GetComponentInParent<BossSplineAttacker>();
        if (splineMovement != null)
        {
            splineMovement.StopAllCoroutines();
            splineMovement.enabled = false;
            if (splineMovement.splineCart != null) splineMovement.splineCart.enabled = false;
        }

        Collider col = GetComponent<Collider>();
        if (col != null) col.enabled = false;

        if (frontHealthBar != null && frontHealthBar.transform.parent != null)
            frontHealthBar.transform.parent.gameObject.SetActive(false);

        GameObject objectToDestroy = splineMovement != null ? splineMovement.gameObject : gameObject;
        Destroy(objectToDestroy, 5f);
    }
}

// [FILE_END]: BossHealth.cs
// ==========================================


// [FILE_START]: BossRamHitbox.cs
// PATH: AI_Core\Boss\BossRamHitbox.cs
// ------------------------------------------

﻿using UnityEngine;

[RequireComponent(typeof(Collider))]
public class BossRamHitbox : MonoBehaviour
{
    [Header("--- RAM DAMAGE SETTINGS ---")]
    public float ramDamage = 60f;
    public float knockbackForce = 25f;

    [Tooltip("VI: Đảm bảo hitbox chỉ va chạm với Player và Môi trường.\nEN: Ensure hitbox only collides with Player and Environment.")]
    public LayerMask targetMask;

    // VI: Hàm kích hoạt khi Đầu Boss tông vào vật thể
    // EN: Function triggered when Boss Head rams an object
    private void OnTriggerEnter(Collider other)
    {
        // VI: Kiểm tra xem vật thể có nằm trong LayerMask cho phép không
        // EN: Check if the object is within the allowed LayerMask
        if (((1 << other.gameObject.layer) & targetMask) == 0) return;

        // ==========================================
        // 1. PHÁ HỦY TỨC THÌ MÔI TRƯỜNG (INSTANT DESTRUCTION)
        // ==========================================
        DestructionBuild destructibleWall = other.GetComponent<DestructionBuild>();
        if (destructibleWall != null)
        {
            // VI: Ép tường nhận một lượng sát thương khổng lồ để vỡ ngay lập tức thay vì trừ từ từ
            // EN: Force the wall to take massive damage to shatter instantly instead of gradually
            IDamageable wallDamageable = other.GetComponent<IDamageable>();
            if (wallDamageable != null)
            {
                wallDamageable.TakeDamage(99999f, transform.position);
            }
            return; // VI: Đã tông vỡ tường thì kết thúc nhịp xử lý này / EN: Wall shattered, end this processing tick
        }

        // ==========================================
        // 2. GÂY SÁT THƯƠNG CHO PLAYER (DAMAGE PLAYER)
        // ==========================================
        IDamageable target = other.GetComponent<IDamageable>();
        if (target == null) target = other.GetComponentInParent<IDamageable>();

        if (target != null)
        {
            // VI: Boss không tự tự sát thương chính mình
            // EN: Boss does not damage itself
            if (other.GetComponent<AIHealth>() != null || other.GetComponent<AIBodyPart>() != null) return;

            // VI: Tính toán hướng hất văng (Đẩy lùi và hất hếch lên trên một chút)
            // EN: Calculate knockback direction (Push back and slightly upwards)
            Vector3 pushDirection = (other.transform.position - transform.position).normalized;
            pushDirection.y = 0.5f;

            // VI: Truyền sát thương (Tùy thuộc vào hàm TakeDamage của Player có nhận thêm Vector3 knockback hay không)
            // EN: Apply damage (Depending on whether Player's TakeDamage accepts a Vector3 knockback)
            target.TakeDamage(ramDamage, transform.position);

            // VI: Gọi hiệu ứng rung màn hình tại đây nếu có
            // EN: Call screen shake effect here if available
        }
    }
}

// [FILE_END]: BossRamHitbox.cs
// ==========================================


// [FILE_START]: BossSplineAttacker.cs
// PATH: AI_Core\Boss\BossSplineAttacker.cs
// ------------------------------------------

﻿using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.Splines;
using Unity.Cinemachine;
using System.Collections;

public class BossSplineAttacker : MonoBehaviour
{
    [Header("--- SYSTEM REFERENCES ---")]
    public SplineContainer splineContainer;
    public CinemachineSplineCart splineCart;
    public Transform playerTarget;

    [Header("--- FLIGHT TRAJECTORY ---")]
    public float emergeDistance = 30f;
    public float undergroundDepth = -15f;
    public float apexHeight = 5f;

    [Header("--- PACING & SPEED ---")]
    public float flightSpeed = 40f;
    public float cooldownBetweenAttacks = 3f;

    private bool _isAttacking = false;

    void Start()
    {
        if (splineContainer.Spline == null) splineContainer.AddSpline();
        splineCart.SplinePosition = 0f;
        StartCoroutine(AttackLoop());
    }

    private IEnumerator AttackLoop()
    {
        while (true)
        {
            yield return new WaitForSeconds(cooldownBetweenAttacks);
            if (playerTarget != null && !_isAttacking)
            {
                CalculateDynamicArc();
                yield return StartCoroutine(ExecuteFlight());
            }
        }
    }

    private void CalculateDynamicArc()
    {
        // ========================================================
        // 1. TÍNH TOÁN TỌA ĐỘ THẾ GIỚI (WORLD SPACE CALCULATION)
        // ========================================================
        Vector3 playerPos = playerTarget.position;

        Vector3 randomRange = Random.insideUnitSphere * emergeDistance;
        randomRange.y = 0;

        Vector3 p0_World = playerPos + randomRange;
        Vector3 p2_World = playerPos - randomRange;

        p0_World.y = undergroundDepth;
        Vector3 p1_World = playerPos + (Vector3.up * apexHeight);
        p2_World.y = undergroundDepth;

        // ========================================================
        // 2. CHUYỂN ĐỔI SANG TỌA ĐỘ CỤC BỘ (CONVERT TO LOCAL SPACE)
        // ========================================================
        Transform splineTransform = splineContainer.transform;
        Vector3 p0_Local = splineTransform.InverseTransformPoint(p0_World);
        Vector3 p1_Local = splineTransform.InverseTransformPoint(p1_World);
        Vector3 p2_Local = splineTransform.InverseTransformPoint(p2_World);

        // ========================================================
        // 3. [FIXED] TẠO SPLINE HOÀN TOÀN MỚI ĐỂ XÓA CACHE
        // 3. [FIXED] CREATE COMPLETELY NEW SPLINE TO CLEAR CACHE
        // ========================================================
        Spline newSpline = new Spline();

        newSpline.Add(new BezierKnot(p0_Local));
        newSpline.Add(new BezierKnot(p1_Local));
        newSpline.Add(new BezierKnot(p2_Local));

        newSpline.SetTangentMode(new SplineRange(0, newSpline.Count), TangentMode.AutoSmooth);

        // VI: Hành động gán này ép CinemachineSplineCart phải tính toán lại đường ray!
        // EN: This assignment action forces the CinemachineSplineCart to recalculate the track!
        splineContainer.Spline = newSpline;
    }

    private IEnumerator ExecuteFlight()
    {
        _isAttacking = true;

        // VI: Tính toán thời gian bay dựa trên vận tốc
        // EN: Calculate flight time based on speed
        float pathLength = splineContainer.CalculateLength();
        float travelTime = pathLength / flightSpeed;

        float normalizedTime = 0f;

        // VI: Vòng lặp đơn giản chạy Cart từ 0.0 đến 1.0 (0% đến 100%)
        // EN: Simple loop running Cart from 0.0 to 1.0 (0% to 100%)
        while (normalizedTime < 1f)
        {
            normalizedTime += Time.deltaTime / travelTime;
            splineCart.SplinePosition = normalizedTime;
            yield return null;
        }

        _isAttacking = false;
    }
}

// [FILE_END]: BossSplineAttacker.cs
// ==========================================


// [FILE_START]: SoundTester.cs
// PATH: AI_Core\Test\SoundTester.cs
// ------------------------------------------

﻿using UnityEngine;

public class SoundTester : MonoBehaviour
{
    [Header("--- THÔNG SỐ TIẾNG ĐỘNG TỰ ĐỘNG ---")]
    [Tooltip("Bán kính lan truyền của tiếng động (mét)")]
    public float soundRadius = 12f;

    [Tooltip("Cứ sau bao nhiêu giây thì tự phát ra tiếng động 1 lần")]
    public float intervalTime = 5f;

    [Header("--- VÙNG PHÁT TIẾNG ĐỘNG NGẪU NHIÊN ---")]
    [Tooltip("Bán kính vùng ngẫu nhiên xung quanh vị trí của Object này để tạo tiếng động")]
    public float spawnRange = 10f;

    private float _nextSoundTime;

    void Start()
    {
        // Khởi tạo nhịp thời gian phát đầu tiên
        _nextSoundTime = Time.time + intervalTime;
    }

    void Update()
    {
        // TUYỆT ĐỐI KHÔNG DÙNG INPUT GET MOUSE / GET KEY CHỐNG LỖI XUNG ĐỘT INPUT SYSTEM
        if (Time.time >= _nextSoundTime)
        {
            _nextSoundTime = Time.time + intervalTime;
            TriggerAutomatedSound();
        }
    }

    private void TriggerAutomatedSound()
    {
        // Tính toán một tọa độ ngẫu nhiên trên mặt phẳng XZ xung quanh Object này
        Vector2 randomCircle = Random.insideUnitCircle * spawnRange;
        Vector3 soundPosition = new Vector3(
            transform.position.x + randomCircle.x,
            transform.position.y,
            transform.position.z + randomCircle.y
        );

        // Bắn sự kiện âm thanh tĩnh ngầm vào bộ não AI của toàn bầy đàn
        AIAuditorySystem.ReportSound(soundPosition, soundRadius);

        // Vẽ vòng tròn giả lập tia màu đỏ trong Scene view để Designer quan sát cự ly âm thanh
        DrawVisualSoundCircle(soundPosition, soundRadius);

        Debug.Log($"<color=yellow>[AUTO SOUND]</color> Đã tự động kích hoạt tiếng ồn {soundRadius}m tại tọa độ: {soundPosition}");
    }

    // Hàm phụ trợ vẽ vòng tròn bằng Debug.DrawLine để sếp dễ căn cự ly trên Scene
    private void DrawVisualSoundCircle(Vector3 center, float radius)
    {
        int segments = 20;
        float angleStep = 360f / segments;
        Vector3 prevPoint = center + new Vector3(radius, 0, 0);

        for (int i = 1; i <= segments; i++)
        {
            float angle = i * angleStep * Mathf.Deg2Rad;
            Vector3 nextPoint = center + new Vector3(Mathf.Cos(angle) * radius, 0, Mathf.Sin(angle) * radius);

            // Vẽ đường nối trong 3 giây rồi biến mất
            Debug.DrawLine(prevPoint, nextPoint, Color.red, 3f);
            prevPoint = nextPoint;
        }
    }
}

// [FILE_END]: SoundTester.cs
// ==========================================


// [FILE_START]: BaseDomainBuilder.cs
// PATH: Fluid-HTN\BaseDomainBuilder.cs
// ------------------------------------------

﻿using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using FluidHTN.Compounds;
using FluidHTN.Conditions;
using FluidHTN.Effects;
using FluidHTN.Factory;
using FluidHTN.Operators;
using FluidHTN.PrimitiveTasks;

namespace FluidHTN
{
    public abstract class BaseDomainBuilder<DB, T>
        where DB : BaseDomainBuilder<DB, T>
        where T : IContext
    {
        // ========================================================= FIELDS

        protected readonly Domain<T> _domain;
        protected List<ITask> _pointers;
        protected readonly IFactory _factory;

        // ========================================================= CONSTRUCTION

        public BaseDomainBuilder(string domainName, IFactory factory)
        {
            _factory = factory;
            _domain = new Domain<T>(domainName);
            _pointers = _factory.CreateList<ITask>();
            _pointers.Add(_domain.Root);
        }

        // ========================================================= PROPERTIES

        public ITask Pointer
        {
            get
            {
                if (_pointers.Count == 0)
                {
                    return null;
                }

                return _pointers[_pointers.Count - 1];
            }
        }

        // ========================================================= HIERARCHY HANDLING

        /// <summary>
        ///     Compound tasks are where HTN get their “hierarchical” nature. You can think of a compound task as 
        ///     a high level task that has multiple ways of being accomplished. There are primarily two types of 
        ///     compound tasks. Selectors and Sequencers. A Selector must be able to decompose a single sub-task, 
        ///     while a Sequence must be able to decompose all its sub-tasks successfully for itself to have decomposed 
        ///     successfully. There is nothing stopping you from extending this toolset with RandomSelect, UtilitySelect,
        ///     etc. These tasks are decomposed until we're left with only Primitive Tasks, which represent a final plan. 
        ///     Compound tasks are comprised of a set of subtasks and a set of conditions.
        ///     http://www.gameaipro.com/GameAIPro/GameAIPro_Chapter12_Exploring_HTN_Planners_through_Example.pdf
        /// </summary>
        /// <typeparam name="P">The type of compound task</typeparam>
        /// <param name="name">The name given to the task, mainly for debug/display purposes</param>
        /// <returns></returns>
        public DB CompoundTask<P>(string name) where P : ICompoundTask, new()
        {
            var parent = new P();
            return CompoundTask(name, parent);
        }

        /// <summary>
        ///     Compound tasks are where HTN get their “hierarchical” nature. You can think of a compound task as 
        ///     a high level task that has multiple ways of being accomplished. There are primarily two types of 
        ///     compound tasks. Selectors and Sequencers. A Selector must be able to decompose a single sub-task, 
        ///     while a Sequence must be able to decompose all its sub-tasks successfully for itself to have decomposed 
        ///     successfully. There is nothing stopping you from extending this toolset with RandomSelect, UtilitySelect,
        ///     etc. These tasks are decomposed until we're left with only Primitive Tasks, which represent a final plan. 
        ///     Compound tasks are comprised of a set of subtasks and a set of conditions.
        ///     http://www.gameaipro.com/GameAIPro/GameAIPro_Chapter12_Exploring_HTN_Planners_through_Example.pdf
        /// </summary>
        /// <typeparam name="P">The type of compound task</typeparam>
        /// <param name="name">The name given to the task, mainly for debug/display purposes</param>
        /// <param task="task">The task instance</param>
        /// <returns></returns>
        public DB CompoundTask<P>(string name, P task) where P : ICompoundTask
        {
            if (task != null)
            {
                if (Pointer is ICompoundTask compoundTask)
                {
                    task.Name = name;
                    _domain.Add(compoundTask, task);
                    _pointers.Add(task);
                }
                else
                {
                    throw new Exception(
                        "Pointer is not a compound task type. Did you forget an End() after a Primitive Task Action was defined?");
                }
            }
            else
            {
                throw new ArgumentNullException(
                    "task");
            }

            return (DB) this;
        }

        /// <summary>
        ///     Primitive tasks represent a single step that can be performed by our AI. A set of primitive tasks is 
        ///     the plan that we are ultimately getting out of the HTN. Primitive tasks are comprised of an operator, 
        ///     a set of effects, a set of conditions and a set of executing conditions.
        ///     http://www.gameaipro.com/GameAIPro/GameAIPro_Chapter12_Exploring_HTN_Planners_through_Example.pdf
        /// </summary>
        /// <typeparam name="P">The type of primitive task</typeparam>
        /// <param name="name">The name given to the task, mainly for debug/display purposes</param>
        /// <returns></returns>
        public DB PrimitiveTask<P>(string name) where P : IPrimitiveTask, new()
        {
            if (Pointer is ICompoundTask compoundTask)
            {
                var parent = new P { Name = name };
                _domain.Add(compoundTask, parent);
                _pointers.Add(parent);
            }
            else
            {
                throw new Exception(
                    "Pointer is not a compound task type. Did you forget an End() after a Primitive Task Action was defined?");
            }

            return (DB) this;
        }

        /// <summary>
        ///     Partial planning is one of the most powerful features of HTN. In simplest terms, it allows
        ///     the planner the ability to not fully decompose a complete plan. HTN is able to do this because
        ///     it uses forward decomposition or forward search to find plans. That is, the planner starts with
        ///     the current world state and plans forward in time from that. This allows the planner to only
        ///     plan ahead a few steps.
        ///     http://www.gameaipro.com/GameAIPro/GameAIPro_Chapter12_Exploring_HTN_Planners_through_Example.pdf
        /// </summary>
        /// <returns></returns>
        protected DB PausePlanTask()
        {
            if (Pointer is IDecomposeAll compoundTask)
            {
                var parent = new PausePlanTask() { Name = "Pause Plan" };
                _domain.Add(compoundTask, parent);
            }
            else
            {
                throw new Exception(
                    "Pointer is not a decompose-all compound task type, like a Sequence. Maybe you tried to Pause Plan a Selector, or forget an End() after a Primitive Task Action was defined?");
            }

            return (DB) this;
        }

        // ========================================================= COMPOUND TASKS

        /// <summary>
        ///     A compound task that requires all sub-tasks to be valid.
        ///     Sub-tasks can be sequences, selectors or actions.
        /// </summary>
        /// <param name="name"></param>
        /// <returns></returns>
        public DB Sequence(string name)
        {
            return CompoundTask<Sequence>(name);
        }

        /// <summary>
        ///     A compound task that requires a single sub-task to be valid.
        ///     Sub-tasks can be sequences, selectors or actions.
        /// </summary>
        /// <param name="name"></param>
        /// <returns></returns>
        public DB Select(string name)
        {
            return CompoundTask<Selector>(name);
        }

        // ========================================================= PRIMITIVE TASKS

        /// <summary>
        ///     A primitive task that can contain conditions, an operator and effects.
        /// </summary>
        /// <param name="name"></param>
        /// <returns></returns>
        public DB Action(string name)
        {
            return PrimitiveTask<PrimitiveTask>(name);
        }

        // ========================================================= CONDITIONS

        /// <summary>
        ///     A precondition is a boolean statement required for the parent task to validate.
        /// </summary>
        /// <param name="name"></param>
        /// <param name="condition"></param>
        /// <returns></returns>
        public DB Condition(string name, Func<T, bool> condition)
        {
            var cond = new FuncCondition<T>(name, condition);
            Pointer.AddCondition(cond);

            return (DB) this;
        }

        /// <summary>
        ///     An executing condition is a boolean statement validated before every call to the current
        ///		primitive task's operator update tick. It's only supported inside primitive tasks / Actions.
        ///		Note that this condition is never validated during planning, only during execution.
        /// </summary>
        /// <param name="name"></param>
        /// <param name="condition"></param>
        /// <returns></returns>
        public DB ExecutingCondition(string name, Func<T, bool> condition)
        {
            if (Pointer is IPrimitiveTask task)
            {
                var cond = new FuncCondition<T>(name, condition);
                task.AddExecutingCondition(cond);
            }
            else
            {
                throw new Exception("Tried to add an Executing Condition, but the Pointer is not a Primitive Task!");
            }

            return (DB) this;
        }

        // ========================================================= OPERATORS

        /// <summary>
        ///     The operator of an Action / primitive task.
        /// </summary>
        public DB Do(Func<T, TaskStatus> action, Func<T, TaskStatus> start = null, Action<T> forceStopAction = null)
        {
            if (Pointer is IPrimitiveTask task)
            {
                var op = new FuncOperator<T>(func: action, start: start, funcStop: forceStopAction);
                task.SetOperator(op);
            }
            else
            {
                throw new Exception("Tried to add an Operator, but the Pointer is not a Primitive Task!");
            }

            return (DB) this;
        }

        // ========================================================= EFFECTS

        /// <summary>
        ///     Effects can be added to an Action / primitive task.
        /// </summary>
        /// <param name="name"></param>
        /// <param name="effectType"></param>
        /// <param name="action"></param>
        /// <returns></returns>
        public DB Effect(string name, EffectType effectType, Action<T, EffectType> action)
        {
            if (Pointer is IPrimitiveTask task)
            {
                var effect = new ActionEffect<T>(name, effectType, action);
                task.AddEffect(effect);
            }
            else
            {
                throw new Exception("Tried to add an Effect, but the Pointer is not a Primitive Task!");
            }

            return (DB) this;
        }

        // ========================================================= OTHER OPERANDS

        /// <summary>
        ///     Every task encapsulation must end with a call to End(), otherwise subsequent calls will be applied wrong.
        /// </summary>
        /// <returns></returns>
        public DB End()
        {
            _pointers.RemoveAt(_pointers.Count - 1);
            return (DB) this;
        }

        /// <summary>
        ///     We can splice multiple domains together, allowing us to define reusable sub-domains.
        /// </summary>
        /// <param name="domain"></param>
        /// <returns></returns>
        public DB Splice(Domain<T> domain)
        {
            if (Pointer is ICompoundTask compoundTask)
            {
                _domain.Add(compoundTask, domain.Root);
            }
            else
            {
                throw new Exception(
                    "Pointer is not a compound task type. Did you forget an End()?");
            }

            return (DB) this;
        }

        /// <summary>
        ///     The identifier associated with a slot can be used to splice 
        ///     sub-domains onto the domain, and remove them, at runtime.
        ///     Use TrySetSlotDomain and ClearSlot on the domain instance at
        ///     runtime to manage this feature. SlotId can typically be implemented
        ///     as an enum.
        /// </summary>
        public DB Slot(int slotId)
        {
            if (Pointer is ICompoundTask compoundTask)
            {
                var slot = new Slot() { SlotId = slotId, Name = $"Slot {slotId}" };
                _domain.Add(compoundTask, slot);
            }
            else
            {
                throw new Exception(
                    "Pointer is not a compound task type. Did you forget an End()?");
            }

            return (DB) this;
        }

        /// <summary>
        ///     We can add a Pause Plan when in a sequence in our domain definition,
        ///     and this will give us partial planning.
        ///     It means that we can tell our planner to only plan up to a certain point,
        ///     then stop. If the partial plan completes execution successfully, the next
        ///     time we try to find a plan, we will continue planning where we left off.
        ///     Typical use cases is to split after we navigate toward a location, since
        ///     this is often time consuming, it's hard to predict the world state when
        ///     we have reached the destination, and thus there's little point wasting
        ///     milliseconds on planning further into the future at that point. We might
        ///     still want to plan what to do when reaching the destination, however, and
        ///     this is where partial plans come into play.
        /// </summary>
        public DB PausePlan()
        {
            return PausePlanTask();
        }

        /// <summary>
        ///     Build the designed domain and return a domain instance.
        /// </summary>
        /// <returns></returns>
        public Domain<T> Build()
        {
            if (Pointer != _domain.Root)
            {
                throw new Exception($"The domain definition lacks one or more End() statements. Pointer is '{Pointer.Name}', but expected '{_domain.Root.Name}'.");
            }

            _factory.FreeList(ref _pointers);

            return _domain;
        }
    }
}


// [FILE_END]: BaseDomainBuilder.cs
// ==========================================


// [FILE_START]: Domain.cs
// PATH: Fluid-HTN\Domain.cs
// ------------------------------------------

﻿using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using FluidHTN.Compounds;

namespace FluidHTN
{
    public class Domain<T> : IDomain where T : IContext
    {
        // ========================================================= FIELDS

        private Dictionary<int, Slot> _slots = null;

        // ========================================================= CONSTRUCTION

        public Domain(string name)
        {
            Root = new TaskRoot { Name = name, Parent = null };
        }
        // ========================================================= PROPERTIES

        public TaskRoot Root { get; }

        // ========================================================= HIERARCHY HANDLING

        public void Add(ICompoundTask parent, ITask subtask)
        {
            if (parent == subtask)
            {
                throw new Exception("Parent-task and Sub-task can't be the same instance!");
            }

            parent.AddSubtask(subtask);
            subtask.Parent = parent;
        }

        public void Add(ICompoundTask parent, Slot slot)
        {
            if (_slots != null)
            {
                if (_slots.ContainsKey(slot.SlotId))
                {
                    throw new Exception("This slot id already exist in the domain definition!");
                }
            }

            parent.AddSubtask(slot);
            slot.Parent = parent;

            if(_slots == null)
            {
                _slots = new Dictionary<int, Slot>();
            }

            _slots.Add(slot.SlotId, slot);
        }

        // ========================================================= PLANNING

        public DecompositionStatus FindPlan(T ctx, out Queue<ITask> plan)
        {
            if (ctx.IsInitialized == false)
            {
                throw new Exception("Context was not initialized!");
            }

            if (ctx.MethodTraversalRecord == null)
            {
                throw new Exception("We require the Method Traversal Record to have a valid instance.");
            }

            ctx.ContextState = ContextState.Planning;

            plan = null;
            var status = DecompositionStatus.Rejected;

            // We first check whether we have a stored start task. This is true
            // if we had a partial plan pause somewhere in our plan, and we now
            // want to continue where we left off.
            // If this is the case, we don't erase the MTR, but continue building it.
            // However, if we have a partial plan, but LastMTR is not 0, that means
            // that the partial plan is still running, but something triggered a replan.
            // When this happens, we have to plan from the domain root (we're not
            // continuing the current plan), so that we're open for other plans to replace
            // the running partial plan.
            if (ctx.HasPausedPartialPlan && ctx.LastMTR.Count == 0)
            {
                status = OnPausedPartialPlan(ctx, ref plan, status);
            }
            else
            {
                status = OnReplanDuringPartialPlanning(ctx, ref plan, status);
            }

            // If this MTR equals the last MTR, then we need to double-check whether we ended up
            // just finding the exact same plan. During decomposition each compound task can't check
            // for equality, only for less than, so this case needs to be treated after the fact.
            if (HasFoundSamePlan(ctx))
            {
                plan = null;
                status = DecompositionStatus.Rejected;
            }

            if (HasDecompositionSucceeded(status))
            {
                // Apply permanent world state changes to the actual world state used during plan execution.
                ApplyPermanentWorldStateStackChanges(ctx);
            }
            else
            {
                // Clear away any changes that might have been applied to the stack
                // No changes should be made or tracked further when the plan failed.
                ClearWorldStateStackChanges(ctx);
            }

            ctx.ContextState = ContextState.Executing;
            return status;
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// We first check whether we have a stored start task. This is true
        /// if we had a partial plan pause somewhere in our plan, and we now
        /// want to continue where we left off.
        /// If this is the case, we don't erase the MTR, but continue building it.
        /// However, if we have a partial plan, but LastMTR is not 0, that means
        /// that the partial plan is still running, but something triggered a replan.
        /// When this happens, we have to plan from the domain root (we're not
        /// continuing the current plan), so that we're open for other plans to replace
        /// the running partial plan.
        /// </summary>
        /// <param name="ctx"></param>
        /// <param name="plan"></param>
        /// <param name="status"></param>
        /// <returns></returns>
        private DecompositionStatus OnReplanDuringPartialPlanning(T ctx, ref Queue<ITask> plan, DecompositionStatus status)
        {
            var lastPartialPlanQueue = CacheLastPartialPlan(ctx);

            ClearMethodTraversalRecord(ctx);

            // Replan through decomposition of the hierarchy
            status = Root.Decompose(ctx, 0, out plan);

            if (HasDecompositionFailed(status))
            {
                RestoreLastPartialPlan(ctx, lastPartialPlanQueue, status);
            }

            return status;
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// If there is a paused partial plan, we cache it to a last partial plan queue.
        /// This is useful when we want to perform a replan, but don't know yet if it will
        /// win over the current plan.
        /// </summary>
        /// <param name="ctx"></param>
        /// <returns></returns>
        private Queue<PartialPlanEntry> CacheLastPartialPlan(T ctx)
        {
            if (ctx.HasPausedPartialPlan == false)
            {
                return null;
            }

            ctx.HasPausedPartialPlan = false;
            var lastPartialPlanQueue = ctx.Factory.CreateQueue<PartialPlanEntry>();

            while (ctx.PartialPlanQueue.Count > 0)
            {
                lastPartialPlanQueue.Enqueue(ctx.PartialPlanQueue.Dequeue());
            }

            return lastPartialPlanQueue;

        }

        /// <summary>
        /// If we failed to find a new plan, we have to restore the old plan,
        /// if it was a partial plan.
        /// </summary>
        /// <param name="ctx"></param>
        /// <param name="lastPartialPlanQueue"></param>
        /// <param name="status"></param>
        private void RestoreLastPartialPlan(T ctx, Queue<PartialPlanEntry> lastPartialPlanQueue, DecompositionStatus status)
        {
            if (lastPartialPlanQueue == null)
            {
                return;
            }

            ctx.HasPausedPartialPlan = true;
            ctx.PartialPlanQueue.Clear();

            while (lastPartialPlanQueue.Count > 0)
            {
                ctx.PartialPlanQueue.Enqueue(lastPartialPlanQueue.Dequeue());
            }

            ctx.Factory.FreeQueue(ref lastPartialPlanQueue);
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// We only erase the MTR if we start from the root task of the domain.
        /// </summary>
        /// <param name="ctx"></param>
        private void ClearMethodTraversalRecord(T ctx)
        {
            ctx.MethodTraversalRecord.Clear();

            if (ctx.DebugMTR)
            {
                ctx.MTRDebug.Clear();
            }
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// If decomposition status is failed or rejected, the replan failed.
        /// </summary>
        /// <param name="status"></param>
        /// <returns></returns>
        private bool HasDecompositionFailed(DecompositionStatus status)
        {
            return status == DecompositionStatus.Rejected || status == DecompositionStatus.Failed;
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// If decomposition status is failed or rejected, the replan failed.
        /// </summary>
        /// <param name="status"></param>
        /// <returns></returns>
        private bool HasDecompositionSucceeded(DecompositionStatus status)
        {
            return status == DecompositionStatus.Succeeded || status == DecompositionStatus.Partial;
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// We first check whether we have a stored start task. This is true
        /// if we had a partial plan pause somewhere in our plan, and we now
        /// want to continue where we left off.
        /// If this is the case, we don't erase the MTR, but continue building it.
        /// </summary>
        /// <param name="ctx"></param>
        /// <param name="plan"></param>
        /// <param name="status"></param>
        /// <returns></returns>
        private DecompositionStatus OnPausedPartialPlan(T ctx, ref Queue<ITask> plan, DecompositionStatus status)
        {
            ctx.HasPausedPartialPlan = false;
            while (ctx.PartialPlanQueue.Count > 0)
            {
                var kvp = ctx.PartialPlanQueue.Dequeue();
                if (plan == null)
                {
                    status = kvp.Task.Decompose(ctx, kvp.TaskIndex, out plan);
                }
                else
                {
                    status = kvp.Task.Decompose(ctx, kvp.TaskIndex, out var subPlan);
                    if (HasDecompositionSucceeded(status))
                    {
                        EnqueueToExistingPlan(ref plan, subPlan);
                    }
                }

                // While continuing a partial plan, we might encounter
                // a new pause.
                if (ctx.HasPausedPartialPlan)
                {
                    break;
                }
            }

            // If we failed to continue the paused partial plan,
            // then we have to start planning from the root.
            if (HasDecompositionFailed(status))
            {
                ClearMethodTraversalRecord(ctx);

                status = Root.Decompose(ctx, 0, out plan);
            }

            return status;
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Enqueues the sub plan's queue onto the existing plan
        /// </summary>
        /// <param name="plan"></param>
        /// <param name="subPlan"></param>
        private void EnqueueToExistingPlan(ref Queue<ITask> plan, Queue<ITask> subPlan)
        {
            while (subPlan.Count > 0)
            {
                plan.Enqueue(subPlan.Dequeue());
            }
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// If this MTR equals the last MTR, then we need to double-check whether we ended up
        /// just finding the exact same plan. During decomposition each compound task can't check
        /// for equality, only for less than, so this case needs to be treated after the fact.
        /// </summary>
        /// <param name="ctx"></param>
        /// <returns></returns>
        private bool HasFoundSamePlan(T ctx)
        {
            var isMTRsEqual = ctx.MethodTraversalRecord.Count == ctx.LastMTR.Count;
            if (isMTRsEqual)
            {
                for (var i = 0; i < ctx.MethodTraversalRecord.Count; i++)
                {
                    if (ctx.MethodTraversalRecord[i] < ctx.LastMTR[i])
                    {
                        isMTRsEqual = false;
                        break;
                    }
                }

                return isMTRsEqual;
            }

            return false;
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Apply permanent world state changes to the actual world state used during plan execution.
        /// </summary>
        /// <param name="ctx"></param>
        private void ApplyPermanentWorldStateStackChanges(T ctx)
        {
            // Trim away any plan-only or plan&execute effects from the world state change stack, that only
            // permanent effects on the world state remains now that the planning is done.
            ctx.TrimForExecution();
            
            for (int i = 0; i < ctx.WorldStateChangeStack.Length; i++)
            {
                var stack = ctx.WorldStateChangeStack[i];
                if (stack != null && stack.Count > 0)
                {
                    ctx.WorldState[i] = stack.Peek().Value;
                    stack.Clear();
                }
            }
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Clear away any changes that might have been applied to the stack
        /// </summary>
        /// <param name="ctx"></param>
        private void ClearWorldStateStackChanges(T ctx)
        {
            foreach (var stack in ctx.WorldStateChangeStack)
            {
                if (stack != null && stack.Count > 0)
                {
                    stack.Clear();
                }
            }
        }

        // ========================================================= SLOTS

        /// <summary>
        ///     At runtime, set a sub-domain to the slot with the given id.
        ///     This can be used with Smart Objects, to extend the behavior
        ///     of an agent at runtime.
        /// </summary>
        public bool TrySetSlotDomain(int slotId, Domain<T> subDomain)
        {
            if(_slots != null && _slots.TryGetValue(slotId, out var slot))
            {
                return slot.Set(subDomain.Root);
            }

            return false;
        }

        /// <summary>
        ///     At runtime, clear the sub-domain from the slot with the given id.
        ///     This can be used with Smart Objects, to extend the behavior
        ///     of an agent at runtime.
        /// </summary>
        public void ClearSlot(int slotId)
        {
            if (_slots != null && _slots.TryGetValue(slotId, out var slot))
            {
                slot.Clear();
            }
        }
    }
}


// [FILE_END]: Domain.cs
// ==========================================

// ------------------------------------------

﻿using FluidHTN.Factory;

namespace FluidHTN
{
    /// <summary>
    ///     A simple domain builder for easy use when one just need the core functionality
    ///     of the BaseDomainBuilder. This class is sealed, so if you want to extend the
    ///     functionality of the domain builder, extend BaseDomainBuilder instead.
    /// </summary>
    /// <typeparam name="T"></typeparam>
    public sealed class DomainBuilder<T> : BaseDomainBuilder<DomainBuilder<T>, T>
        where T : IContext
    {
        // ========================================================= CONSTRUCTION

        public DomainBuilder(string domainName) : base(domainName, new DefaultFactory())
        {
        }

        public DomainBuilder(string domainName, IFactory factory) : base(domainName, factory)
        {
        }
    }
}

// [FILE_START]: FuncCondition.cs
// PATH: Fluid-HTN\Conditions\FuncCondition.cs
// ------------------------------------------

﻿using System;

namespace FluidHTN.Conditions
{
    public class FuncCondition<T> : ICondition where T : IContext
    {
        // ========================================================= FIELDS

        private readonly Func<T, bool> _func;

        // ========================================================= CONSTRUCTION

        public FuncCondition(string name, Func<T, bool> func)
        {
            Name = name;
            _func = func;
        }

        // ========================================================= PROPERTIES

        public string Name { get; }

        // ========================================================= VALIDITY

        public bool IsValid(IContext ctx)
        {
            if (ctx is T c)
            {
                var result = _func?.Invoke(c) ?? false;

                if (ctx.LogDecomposition)
                {
                    ctx.Log(Name, $"FuncCondition.IsValid:{result}", ctx.CurrentDecompositionDepth+1, this, result ? ConsoleColor.DarkGreen : ConsoleColor.DarkRed);
                }

                return result;
            }

            throw new Exception("Unexpected context type!");
        }
    }
}
